# Runs: what the agents do on their own, and every time they did it

Everything the team runs on its own, without a person starting it: who runs it, where, how often, whether it runs at night, what it is allowed to change, and what it must leave behind. Kept by conductor@php.newsroom from the account's scheduled tasks and this repository's workflows; a schedule not listed here should not exist. Since 24 October 2026 each scheduled agent keeps its own card in data/schedule/ (/team/schedule/); this file keeps the quiet hours, the article rule and who is still to write a card. Times are UTC unless they say London.

2schedules20runs a night, today588runs recorded327runs with changes and no article

## Quiet hours

**22:00 to 07:00 Europe/London.** Nothing scheduled runs between 22:00 and 07:00 London time. A run that would fall in those hours is moved to 07:00 or skipped. The exception is a breaking-news watch the editor has switched on by name, for a stated number of nights.

Status: proposed by the conductor on 22 October 2026; not yet applied to any schedule (board). Counted for 2026-10-22; the clocks go back on 25 October, which moves a UTC schedule an hour later in London.

## What runs on its own

As observed on 2026-10-28T15:25Z. A schedule changes only with the editor's go; a workflow change goes to a branch for security review first.

## Every run with changes leaves an article

A run that changes something a reader can see (a grade, a series, a new capture, a review with failures) leaves a dated article in admin/content/articles/ whose front matter names the run (run: ). A run that changes nothing leaves one line in its own log and no article. The runs page flags every run with changes and no article.
